Voice 1
1. Yaar aaj to mera poora din hi ajeeb guzra hai.
2. Subah uthne ka bilkul dil nahi kar raha tha, bari mushkil se utha aur phir jaldi jaldi tayyar ho kar university chala gaya.
3. Wahan pohancha to pehli class hi shuru hone wali thi, bas kisi tarah andar ja kar baith gaya.
4. Phir do classes ke baad thora free time mila to main aur Ali canteen chale gaye, wahan baith kar chai pi aur kaafi dair tak gupain marte rahe.
5. Us ke baad ek aur class thi lekin sach bataun to us mein bilkul dhyan nahi lag raha tha.
6. Abhi ghar aaya hoon aur soch raha hoon thori dair aaram kar loon, phir raat ko apna kaam mukammal karoon ga kyun ke kal bhi kuch jama karwana hai.or tun suna sab theek thak.

Voice 2
1. Yaar sun, kal ka kya program hai?
2. Main soch raha tha ke agar tu free hai to kahin bahar chalte hain.
3. Kaafi din ho gaye hain bas university aur ghar ke chakkar mein hi waqt guzar raha hai.
4. Kal waise bhi koi khaas kaam nahi hai, is liye socha thora bahar baithenge, kuch khayenge aur aaram se gupain marenge.
5. Ali ko bhi bula lete hain, woh bhi kaafi din se keh raha tha ke kahin chalte hain.
6. Agar tu subah jaldi uth sakta hai to gyarah barah baje nikal jate hain, pehle nashta kar lenge phir dekhenge aage kahan jana hai.
7. Bas tu mujhe aaj hi bata dena taake main bhi apna baqi kaam pehle khatam kar loon.yaad say bata dein bhool na jayn.
8. chal shii hay phir.

Voice 3
1. Oye yaar kahan hai tu?
2. Kaafi dair se message kar raha hoon, jawab hi nahi de raha.
3. Main soch raha tha aaj shaam ko kahin baithte hain, kaafi din ho gaye hain aaram se baat hi nahi hui.
4. University mein bhi milte hain to bas jaldi jaldi salam dua hoti hai aur phir har koi apne kaam mein lag jata hai.
5. Waise kal jo baat kar raha tha na, woh kaam ho gaya ya abhi tak para hua hai?
6. Agar nahi hua to aaj raat ko kar lena, aakhri waqt par hamesha masla ban jata hai.
7. Aur haan, agar shaam ko bahar nikla to mujhe bata dena, main bhi ghar se nikal aaunga.
8. Kuch kha lenge aur thori dair baithenge.

Voice 4
1. Yaar aaj to raste mein ajeeb hi traffic thi.
2. Main soch kar nikla tha ke aaram se waqt par pohanch jaunga, lekin aadhe raste mein aisi traffic mili ke bas pooch mat.
3. Ek jagah to taqreeban das minute gaari wahin khari rahi, aage kuch pata hi nahi chal raha tha ke hua kya hai.
4. Main ne socha shayad koi accident hua hai lekin baad mein pata chala ke bas log sarak ke beech mein gaariyan laga kar kharay thay.
5. Bari mushkil se rasta nikla aur phir university pohancha to class shuru huay bhi pandrah minute ho chuke thay.
6. Sir ne bhi dekh kar bas itna kaha ke aaj phir dair se aaye ho.
7. Main ne kaha sir traffic bohat thi, ab is se zyada kya kehta.

Voice 5
1. Bhai aaj canteen mein itna rush tha ke baithne ki jagah hi nahi mil rahi thi.
2. Main aur Ali kaafi dair tak idhar udhar ghoomte rahe, phir aakhir mein ek table khaali hui to ja kar baithay.
3. Main ne socha chai pee lete hain lekin chai aane mein bhi pandrah minute lag gaye.
4. Ali to waise hi bhooka tha, us ne burger aur fries bhi mangwa liye.
5. Main ne kaha yaar itna kuch mat kha, baad mein class mein neend aaye gi.

7. Phir hum dono taqreeban aadhi class ke baad hi andar gaye.

Voice 6
1. Yaar ek baat bata, jo assignment sir ne diya tha na woh samajh aaya tujhe?

3. Phir socha aaj kisi se pooch kar kar loon ga.
4. Waise Ali keh raha tha ke us ne aadha kaam kar liya hai, agar us ka ho gaya to us se bhi dekh loon ga.
5. Masla ye hai ke deadline bhi qareeb aa gayi hai aur abhi tak mera kuch khaas nahi hua.

Voice 7

Voice 26 — Dost ka der se aana

Voice 27 — Market mein khareedari
1. Bhai, aaj main market gaya tha kuch zaroori cheezein khareedne ke liye, lekin wahan ja kar mujhe andaza hua ke har cheez kitni mehngi ho gayi hai.
2. Jis cheez ki qeemat pichli dafa paanch sau rupay thi, aaj dukandaar saat sau maang raha tha.
3. Main ne us se kaha ke bhai, itni bhi kya mehngai hai, thora munasib rate laga do.
4. Pehle to woh maan hi nahi raha tha, phir kaafi behas ke baad sau rupay kam kar diye.
5. Waise mera khayal hai ke market janay se pehle cheezon ki qeematein check kar leni chahiye taake dukandaar zyada paise na le.
6. Ek aur baat, agar do teen dukaanon se rate pooch lo to kaafi farq pata chal jata hai.

1. Yaar pehle mujhe lagta tha ke thoray thoray paise bachane se koi khaas farq nahi parta, lekin ab samajh aa rahi hai ke bachat karna kitna zaroori hai.
2. Jab bhi jaib mein paise aate hain to dil karta hai ke kuch kha loon, doston ke saath bahar chala jaoon ya koi nayi cheez khareed loon.
3. Phir mahinay ke aakhir mein hisaab lagata hoon to pata chalta hai ke kaafi paise ghair zaroori cheezon par kharch ho gaye.
4. Ab main koshish karta hoon ke kharch karne se pehle sochoon ke yeh cheez waqai zaroori hai ya sirf dil kar raha hai.

Voice 54 — Safar ki tayari
1. Bhai jab bhi kahin safar par jana ho to main ek din pehle hi tayari shuru kar deta hoon.
2. Sab se pehle kapray nikalta hoon, phir zaroori cheezen bag mein rakhta hoon aur aakhir mein dekhta hoon ke mobile ka charger saath hai ya nahi.
3. Ek dafa main jaldi mein charger ghar bhool gaya tha aur poore safar mein mobile ki battery khatam hone ki fikar lagi rahi.
4. Ab main koshish karta hoon ke safar se pehle ek chhoti si fehrist bana loon taake koi cheez reh na jaye.
5. Waise safar mein khirki wali seat mil jaye to maza dobala ho jata hai.

Voice 55 — Nayi maharat seekhna
1. Yaar aaj kal main soch raha hoon ke koi nayi maharat seekhni chahiye kyun ke sirf waqt guzarne se kuch hasil nahi hota.
2. Shuru mein jab koi nayi cheez seekhte hain to kaafi mushkil lagti hai aur kai dafa dil karta hai ke chhor do, yeh tum se nahi hoga.
3. Lekin agar roz thora sa waqt nikaal kar mashq ki jaye to aahista aahista cheezen samajh aane lagti hain.
4. Zaroori nahi ke ek hi din mein sab kuch seekh liya jaye.
5. Asal baat yeh hai ke insaan koshish jari rakhe, ghaltiyon se seekhe aur apni pichhli karkardagi se behtar hone ki koshish kare.

Voice 56 — Doston ke saath cricket
1. Yaar kal hum sab dost kaafi arsay baad cricket khelne gaye thay.
2. Shuru mein to sab baray josh mein thay aur har koi keh raha tha ke aaj main sab se zyada runs banaunga.
3. Jab khel shuru hua to pehle hi over mein hamari team ke do khilari out ho gaye.
4. Phir sab ek doosray ko mashwaray dene lagay ke batting kaise karni hai.
5. Match kaafi dilchasp tha aur aakhir mein sirf do runs ka farq reh gaya.
6. Hum match haar gaye, lekin kisi ko zyada afsos nahi hua kyun ke sab ne mil kar khoob maza kiya tha.

Voice 57 — Pasandeeda khana
1. Bhai agar mujh se poochho ke mera pasandeeda khana kya hai to main foran biryani ka naam loonga.
2. Khaas taur par jab ghar mein biryani ban rahi ho to us ki khushboo se hi bhook lagne lagti hai.
3. Kabhi kabhi doston ke saath bahar khanay ka program bhi ban jata hai aur phir hum sab apni apni pasand ki cheezen mangwatay hain.
4. Mujhe lagta hai ke khanay ka maza sirf zaiqay mein nahi hota balkay saath baithnay walay logon mein bhi hota hai.
5. Jab doston ya ghar walon ke saath khana khatay hain to aam sa khana bhi khaas lagne lagta hai.

Voice 58 — University ka din
1. Aaj university mein mera din kaafi masroof guzra.
2. Subah class mein pohncha to ustad pehle hi lecture shuru kar chukay thay, is liye jaldi se apni jagah baith gaya.
3. Pehli class mein ek naya mauzoo parhaya gaya jo shuru mein thora mushkil laga, lekin jab ustad ne misaalon se samjhaya to baat wazeh ho gayi.
4. Break ke dauran doston ke saath canteen par gaye aur chai pi.
5. Phir agli class mein assignment ke baray mein baat hui.
6. Ghar wapas aa kar main ne socha ke aaj ka kaam isi din mukammal kar leta hoon taake kal ke liye pareshani na ho.

Voice 59 — Bijli ki lodashidanag
1. Yaar hamaray ilaqay mein kal achanak bijli chali gayi aur kaafi der tak wapas nahi aayi.
2. Main apna zaroori kaam kar raha tha ke achanak computer band ho gaya.
3. Pehle to mujhe ghussa aaya kyun ke kaam abhi mukammal nahi hua tha, lekin phir socha ke thori der aaram hi kar leta hoon.
4. Ghar mein sab log pankhay ke baghair baithay thay aur garmi bhi kaafi thi.
5. Kuch der baad bijli wapas aayi to sab ne sukoon ka saans liya.
6. Aise waqt mein ehsaas hota hai ke rozmarrah ki chhoti chhoti sahoolatein bhi hamari zindagi mein kitni aham hain.

Voice 60 — Waqt ki pabandi
1. Bhai main ne mehsoos kiya hai ke waqt ki pabandi insaan ki zindagi mein bohat farq daalti hai.
2. Agar hum har kaam aakhri waqt par karne ki aadat bana lein to aksar pareshani ka saamna karna parta hai.
3. Misaal ke taur par kahin jana ho to main pehle tayari shuru nahi karta tha, phir raste mein jaldi hoti thi aur kai dafa der bhi ho jati thi.
4. Ab koshish karta hoon ke ghar se kuch waqt pehle nikloon aur apne kaam pehle se tarteeb de loon.
5. Is tarah zehan par dabao kam hota hai aur baqi kaamon ke liye bhi waqt nikal aata hai.

Voice 61 — Purani yaadein
1. Yaar kabhi kabhi purani tasveerein dekhne baith jao to pata hi nahi chalta ke kitna waqt guzar gaya.
2. Pichlay dinon main ne apne school ke zamanay ki kuch tasveerein dekhin to bohat si baatein yaad aa gayin.
3. Woh dost jin ke saath roz mulaqat hoti thi, woh classroom, woh chhuttiyan aur chhoti chhoti shararatein, sab kuch zehan mein taza ho gaya.
4. Ab sab apni apni zindagi mein masroof hain aur roz milna mumkin nahi hota.
5. Lekin jab kabhi kisi puranay dost se baat ho jaye to aisa lagta hai jaise waqt kuch lamhon ke liye peechay chala gaya ho.

Voice 62 — Ghar ki safai
1. Aaj Ammi ne subah hi keh diya tha ke ghar ki safai mein sab ko haath batana hoga.
2. Main ne socha tha ke thora sa kaam hoga aur jaldi khatam ho jayega, lekin jab samaan tarteeb dena shuru kiya to pata chala ke kaafi cheezen apni jagah par nahi thin.
3. Pehle kamra saaf kiya, phir kitabein tarteeb se rakheen aur aakhir mein mez par para hua sara ghair zaroori samaan hataya.
4. Kaam khatam hua to kamra pehle se zyada saaf aur khula khula lag raha tha.
5. Ab mujhe samajh aati hai ke cheezen apni jagah par rakhne se rozana ka kaam bhi aasaan ho jata hai.

Voice 63 — Nayi technology
1. Aaj kal technology itni tezi se badal rahi hai ke har roz kuch naya dekhne ko milta hai.
2. Pehle log maloomat hasil karne ke liye kitabein talash karte thay ya kisi se poochte thay, lekin ab mobile par chand seconds mein bohat kuch mil jata hai.
3. Computer aur internet ne parhai, karobar aur rabtay ke tareeqay bhi badal diye hain.
4. Lekin mere khayal mein technology istemal karne ke saath yeh samajhna bhi zaroori hai ke kaun si maloomat durust hain aur kaun si ghalat.
5. Har cheez par foran yaqeen nahi karna chahiye balkay soch samajh kar faisla karna chahiye.

Voice 64 — Chai ki dukaan
1. Bhai hamaray mohallay mein ek chhoti si chai ki dukaan hai jahan shaam ke waqt kaafi log jama ho jatay hain.
2. Wahan ki chai zyada mehngi nahi hai, lekin us ka zaiqa bohat acha hai.
3. Kuch log daftar se wapas aa kar chai peete hain, kuch doston ke saath baith kar gup shup karte hain aur kuch bas thori der aaram karne aa jatay hain.
4. Dukaan wala bhi sab ko pehchanta hai aur kai logon ko un ki pasand ki chai baghair poochay bana deta hai.
5. Mujhe aisi jagahen achhi lagti hain jahan log chand minute baith kar rozmarrah ki masroofiyat bhool jatay hain.

Voice 65 — Aik acha din
1. Yaar zaroori nahi ke har acha din kisi baray kaam ya khaas mauqay ki wajah se yaadgar banay.
2. Kabhi kabhi aam sa din bhi bohat acha guzar jata hai, bas ghar ka mahaul acha ho, doston se baat ho jaye aur apne kaam waqt par mukammal ho jayen.
3. Kal mere saath bhi kuch aisa hi hua.
4. Subah main ne apne zaroori kaam mukammal kiye, dopahar ko dost se baat hui aur shaam ko ghar walon ke saath baith kar khana khaya.
5. Koi khaas program nahi tha, lekin din ke aakhir mein dil mutmain tha.
6. Mere khayal mein khush rehne ke liye chhoti chhoti achhi baaton ki qadar karna zaroori hai.

Voice 66 — Subah ki sair
1. Yaar main kaafi dinon se soch raha hoon ke subah jaldi uth kar sair karne jaya karoon.
2. Mere dost ne bataya ke subah ki taza hawa mein chalne se tabiyat achhi rehti hai aur sara din bhi behtar guzarta hai.
3. Masla bas yeh hai ke subah bistar chhorna mere liye kaafi mushkil kaam hai.
4. Ek din main jaldi utha aur qareebi park chala gaya.
5. Wahan kuch log walk kar rahe thay, kuch warzish kar rahe thay aur kuch benchon par baithay baatein kar rahe thay.
6. Mahaul bohat pursukoon tha.
7. Ab soch raha hoon ke roz thora waqt nikaal kar yeh aadat jari rakhun.

Voice 67 — Traffic ka masla
1. Bhai hamaray shehar mein traffic ka masla din ba din barhta ja raha hai.
2. Khaas taur par subah aur shaam ke waqt sarakon par itna rush hota hai ke thora sa faasla tay karne mein bhi kaafi waqt lag jata hai.
3. Log jaldi mein ek doosray ki gaariyon ke aagay nikalne ki koshish karte hain, jis se mazeed maslay paida hotay hain.
4. Kai dafa main bhi waqt par pohanchne ke liye ghar se jaldi nikalta hoon, lekin raste mein traffic ki wajah se der ho jati hai.
5. Mere khayal mein agar log traffic qawaneen ki pabandi karein aur sabr se kaam lein to halaat behtar ho sakte hain.

Voice 68 — Kitaab parhne ka shauq
1. Yaar pehle mujhe kitabein parhne mein zyada dilchaspi nahi thi.
2. Mujhe lagta tha ke kitaab parhne ke bajaye koi video dekh lena zyada aasaan hai.
3. Phir ek din mere dost ne mujhe ek dilchasp kitaab di aur kaha ke ise chand safhay parh kar dekho.
4. Main ne parhna shuru kiya to kahani itni achhi lagi ke waqt guzarne ka pata hi nahi chala.
5. Is ke baad mujhe ehsaas hua ke kitabein sirf maloomat hi nahi detin balkay insaan ki sochnay ki salahiyat bhi behtar banati hain.
6. Ab koshish karta hoon ke roz kuch na kuch parhun, chahe sirf das minute hi kyun na hon.

Voice 69 — Eid ki tayyariyan
1. Yaar Eid ka naam suntay hi bachpan ki bohat si yaadein taza ho jati hain.
2. Eid se kuch din pehle hi ghar mein tayyariyan shuru ho jati thin.
3. Naye kapray khareedne, jootay lene aur ghar ki safai karne mein sab masroof rehte thay.
4. Eid ki subah jaldi uth kar nahana, achhay kapray pehenna aur sab se pehle Eidi lene ka apna hi maza tha.
5. Phir rishtedaron ke ghar jana aur doston ke saath ghoomna bhi Eid ka hissa hota tha.
6. Ab masroofiyat barh gayi hain, lekin Eid par ghar walon ke saath waqt guzarne ki khushi aaj bhi waisi hi hai.

Voice 70 — Internet ke faiday
1. Bhai internet ne hamari zindagi bohat aasaan bana di hai.
2. Pehle kisi nayi cheez ke baray mein jaanne ke liye kitabein talash karni parti thin ya kisi maahir se poochna parta tha.
3. Ab ghar baithay videos dekh kar, mazameen parh kar aur online courses ke zariye nayi maharatein seekhi ja sakti hain.
4. Log ghar se kaam karte hain, online khareedari karte hain aur door baithay apne rishtedaron se baat bhi kar lete hain.
5. Lekin internet ka istemal karte waqt ehtiyat bhi zaroori hai, kyun ke har website qabil-e-aitemad nahi hoti.
6. Humein apni zaati maloomat mehfooz rakhni chahiye aur har khabar ko sach nahi samajhna chahiye.

Voice 71 — Ghar mein khana pakana
1. Kal main ne socha ke aaj bahar se khana mangwanay ke bajaye ghar mein kuch banaya jaye.
2. Shuru mein mujhe samajh nahi aa rahi thi ke kya pakaoon, phir main ne faisla kiya ke aloo ke parathay banata hoon.
3. Ammi ne mujhe tareeqa samjhaya aur bataya ke aatay mein kitna pani daalna hai aur parathay ko kitni der pakana hai.
4. Pehla paratha thora kharab ban gaya, lekin doosra pehle se behtar tha.
5. Khana tayyar hua to sab ne mil kar khaya.
6. Mujhe acha laga ke main ne khud kuch banane ki koshish ki.
7. Ab irada hai ke aahista aahista mazeed cheezen pakana seekhun.

Voice 72 — Kaam mein ghalti ho jana
1. Yaar kabhi kabhi insaan se kaam karte hue aisi ghalti ho jati hai jis ki wajah se sara kaam dobara karna parta hai.
2. Mere saath bhi pichlay dinon aisa hua.
3. Main ek zaroori file par kaam kar raha tha aur jaldi mein use sahi jagah mehfooz karna bhool gaya.
4. Jab computer band kiya to baad mein file dhoondne mein kaafi waqt lag gaya.
5. Pehle to main pareshan hua, lekin phir main ne socha ke is tajurbe se kuch seekhna chahiye.
6. Ab main kaam mukammal karne ke baad file mehfooz karta hoon aur ek izafi copy bhi rakh leta hoon taake dobara aisa masla na ho.

Voice 73 — Pasandeeda mausam
1. Agar tum mujh se poochho ke mujhe kaun sa mausam pasand hai to main sardiyon ka naam loonga.
2. Sardiyon mein subah ke waqt kambal se nikalne ko dil nahi karta, lekin garam chai peene ka maza bhi alag hota hai.
3. Shaam ko doston ke saath baith kar gup shup karna aur dhoop mein kuch der baithna acha lagta hai.
4. Is mausam mein garam kapray pehenne partay hain, is liye bahar nikalne se pehle kaafi tayari karni hoti hai.
5. Albatta mujhe bohat zyada sardi pasand nahi, kyun ke phir rozmarrah ke kaam karna mushkil ho jata hai.
6. Halki sardi ho to mausam ka maza dobala ho jata hai.

Voice 74 — Kisi ki madad karna
1. Bhai mere khayal mein kisi ki madad karne ke liye hamesha bohat zyada paison ya waqt ki zaroorat nahi hoti.
2. Kabhi kabhi kisi ko rasta bata dena, us ki baat sun lena ya mushkil waqt mein us ke saath kharay rehna bhi bohat bari madad hoti hai.
3. Pichlay dinon main ne ek buzurg ko sarak paar karne mein madad di thi.
4. Unhon ne muskura kar shukriya kaha to mujhe bohat acha mehsoos hua.
5. Is chhotay se kaam mein zyada waqt bhi nahi laga, lekin shayad un ke liye yeh madad aham thi.
6. Humein koshish karni chahiye ke jahan mumkin ho, doosron ke kaam aayen.

Voice 75 — Haftay ki chhutti
1. Yaar poora hafta kaam ya parhai mein guzar jaye to haftay ki chhutti ka sab ko intezar rehta hai.
2. Main bhi sochta hoon ke chhutti walay din der tak soonga aur koi kaam nahi karunga, lekin aksar aisa hota hai ke subah hi ghar ke kai kaam nikal aate hain.
3. Kabhi bazaar jana parta hai, kabhi kamra saaf karna hota hai aur kabhi koi zaroori cheez khareedni hoti hai.
4. Phir bhi koshish karta hoon ke shaam ka waqt apne liye rakhun.
5. Kabhi koi film dekh leta hoon aur kabhi doston ke saath bahar chala jata hoon.
6. Thora aaram aglay haftay ke liye taza dam kar deta hai.

Voice 76 — Acha dost
1. Yaar zindagi mein achhay doston ka hona bohat zaroori hai.
2. Acha dost woh nahi hota jo sirf khushi ke waqt tumharay saath ho, balkay woh hota hai jo mushkil waqt mein bhi tumhein akela na chhoray.
3. Kabhi insaan pareshan hota hai aur use kisi aisay shakhs ki zaroorat hoti hai jo us ki baat sun sakay aur sahi mashwara de sakay.
4. Mere khayal mein dosti mein aitmaad aur izzat bohat aham hain.
5. Har baat par ittefaq karna zaroori nahi, lekin ek doosray ki raye sunni chahiye.
6. Agar dosti mein sachai aur khuloos ho to waqt aur faaslay bhi rishtay ko kamzor nahi kar patay.

Voice 77 — Pasandeeda shehar
1. Yaar mujhe Lahore shehar bohat pasand hai kyun ke yahan ki ronaq, khanay aur tareekhi jagahen apni misaal aap hain.
2. Jab bhi Lahore ke kisi mashhoor ilaqay mein janay ka mauqa milta hai to wahan ka mahaul dekh kar acha lagta hai.
3. Kabhi doston ke saath khanay ka program ban jata hai aur kabhi kisi tareekhi jagah ki sair karne chalay jatay hain.
4. Albatta shehar mein traffic bohat zyada hai aur baaz auqat ek jagah se doosri jagah pohanchne mein kaafi waqt lag jata hai.
5. Phir bhi Lahore ki apni alag pehchan hai.
6. Yahan ke log, bazaar aur khanay is shehar ko khaas banatay hain.

Voice 78 — Subah ka nashta
1. Bhai mere khayal mein din ka aaghaz achhay nashtay se hona chahiye.
2. Jab subah jaldi mein kuch khaye baghair ghar se nikalta hoon to thori der baad hi bhook lagne lagti hai.
3. Phir kaam par tawajjo dena bhi mushkil ho jata hai.
4. Hamare ghar mein kabhi anday aur parathay bantay hain, kabhi chai ke saath biscuit kha letay hain aur kabhi raat ke bachay hue khanay se kaam chala letay hain.
5. Mujhe khaas taur par garam parathay ke saath chai peena pasand hai.
6. Waise nashta hamesha ek jaisa nahi hona chahiye, kabhi kuch naya khanay se bhi din acha shuru hota hai.

Voice 79 — Aik dilchasp film
1. Yaar pichlay haftay main ne ek film dekhi jo mujhe kaafi pasand aayi.
2. Shuru mein mujhe laga tha ke shayad kahani aam si hogi, lekin jaise jaise film aagay barhi, is mein dilchaspi barhti gayi.
3. Is film mein dosti, mehnat aur mushkil halaat ka saamna karne ke baray mein dikhaya gaya tha.
4. Mujhe khaas taur par woh hissa acha laga jahan markazi kirdar ne himmat nahi hari aur apni ghaltiyon se seekh kar dobara koshish ki.
5. Film khatam honay ke baad main kaafi der tak is ke baray mein sochta raha.
6. Mere khayal mein achhi film sirf tafreeh nahi karti balkay insaan ko kuch sochnay ka mauqa bhi deti hai.

Voice 80 — Dukaan se khareedari
1. Kal mujhe ghar ke liye kuch zaroori samaan khareedna tha, is liye main qareebi dukaan par chala gaya.
2. Main ne dukandaar se chand cheezon ki qeemat poochhi to us ne bataya ke kuch cheezen pehle se mehngi ho gayi hain.
3. Main ne apni fehrist nikali aur sirf wohi cheezen khareedeen jin ki waqai zaroorat thi.
4. Pehle main aksar dukaan par ja kar izafi cheezen bhi le leta tha, lekin ab koshish karta hoon ke paise soch samajh kar kharch karoon.
5. Khareedari ke baad ghar wapas aaya to ehsaas hua ke pehle se fehrist bana lene se waqt bhi bachta hai aur ghair zaroori kharch bhi kam hota hai.

Voice 81 — Pasandeeda khel
1. Yaar mujhe bachpan se khailon mein dilchaspi rahi hai, khaas taur par cricket dekhna aur khelna acha lagta hai.
2. Jab Pakistan ka match ho to ghar mein sab log apni apni jagah baith kar match dekhtay hain.
3. Koi har gend par tabsara karta hai, koi khilariyon ki karkardagi par baat karta hai aur koi sirf match ke dilchasp lamhaat ka intezar karta hai.
4. Agar hamari team acha khel rahi ho to sab ka mood bhi acha rehta hai.
5. Lekin jab koi aham khilari jaldi out ho jaye to sab pareshan ho jatay hain.
6. Mere liye khel sirf jeetnay ka naam nahi balkay logon ko ikattha karne ka bhi zariya hai.

Voice 82 — Ghar walon ke saath waqt
1. Yaar aaj kal har koi apni masroofiyat mein itna uljha hua hai ke ghar walon ke saath baithnay ka waqt kam milta hai.
2. Koi parhai mein masroof hai, koi kaam par jata hai aur koi mobile istemal karta rehta hai.
3. Mere khayal mein din mein kuch waqt aisa zaroor hona chahiye jab sab log apne kaam chhor kar ikatthay baithain.
4. Hum ek doosray se poochhein ke din kaisa guzra aur koi nayi baat hui ya nahi.
5. Kabhi kabhi sirf das ya pandrah minute ki guftagu bhi rishton ko mazboot banati hai.
6. Aakhir mein ghar walon ka saath hi insaan ko mushkil waqt mein hosla deta hai.

Voice 83 — Baarish mein safar
1. Bhai barish ke mausam mein safar karna kabhi kabhi bohat mushkil ho jata hai.
2. Sarakon par pani jama ho jata hai, gariyan aahista chalti hain aur kai jagah traffic bhi ruk jati hai.
3. Pichhli baar jab main barish mein ghar se nikla to mujhe laga tha ke aadhe ghantay mein pohanch jaunga, lekin raste mein kaafi der lag gayi.
4. Kapray bhi thoray bheeg gaye aur jooton par kichar lag gayi.
5. Is ke bawajood barish ka apna maza hai, khaas taur par jab aap ghar pohanch jayen aur garam chai mil jaye.
6. Bas safar karte waqt ehtiyat aur izafi waqt rakhna zaroori hai.

Voice 84 — Nayi zaban seekhna
1. Yaar ek nayi zaban seekhna shuru mein mushkil lagta hai, lekin jab roz thori mashq ki jaye to aahista aahista behtari aane lagti hai.
2. Sirf alfaaz yaad karna kaafi nahi hota balkay unhein jumlon mein istemal karna bhi zaroori hai.
3. Main ne dekha hai ke jab kisi zaban mein rozmarrah ki baatein sunne aur bolne ki koshish ki jaye to seekhna aasaan ho jata hai.
4. Ghaltiyan bhi hoti hain, lekin un se ghabranay ke bajaye unhein durust karna chahiye.
5. Zaroori nahi ke shuru se hi bilkul sahi bolein.
6. Asal maqsad yeh hai ke aap musalsal koshish karein aur waqt ke saath apni baat behtar tareeqay se samjha sakein.

Voice 85 — Gaon ki zindagi
1. Yaar gaon ki zindagi shehar se kaafi mukhtalif hoti hai.
2. Wahan subah ke waqt parindon ki aawazein sunai deti hain aur mahaul nisbat-an pursukoon hota hai.
3. Log ek doosray ko achhi tarah jaantay hain aur zaroorat parnay par ek doosray ki madad bhi karte hain.
4. Gaon mein khuli jagahen, khet aur darakht dekh kar acha lagta hai.
5. Albatta baaz ilaqon mein taleem, ilaaj aur rozgar ki sahoolatein shehar jaisi nahi hotin.
6. Isi wajah se bohat se log behtar mauqon ki talaash mein shehron ka rukh karte hain.
7. Mere khayal mein agar gaon mein bhi bunyadi sahoolatein behtar ho jayen to wahan rehnay walon ki zindagi aasaan ho sakti hai.

Voice 86 — Apni ghalti maanna
1. Bhai apni ghalti maanna aasaan nahi hota, khaas taur par jab insaan ko lagta ho ke woh hamesha sahi hota hai.
2. Kabhi hum ghussay mein kisi se aisi baat keh dete hain jis se use dukh pohanchta hai.
3. Baad mein ehsaas hota hai ke humein is tarah baat nahi karni chahiye thi.
4. Mere khayal mein ghalti maan lene se insaan chhota nahi hota balkay us ki shakhsiyat behtar hoti hai.
5. Agar hum kisi ko takleef pohanchayen to maafi maangne mein der nahi karni chahiye.
6. Har insaan se ghaltiyan hoti hain, lekin asal farq is baat se parta hai ke hum apni ghalti se kya seekhtay hain aur aainda apne rawaiye ko kaise behtar banatay hain.

Voice 87 — Subah der se uthna
1. Yaar aaj main kaafi der se utha aur phir poora din jaldi jaldi kaam karta raha.
2. Raat ko mobile istemal karte hue waqt ka andaza hi nahi hua aur sonay mein bhi der ho gayi.
3. Subah alarm baja to main ne use band karke dobara aankhein band kar lein.
4. Jab dobara utha to waqt kaafi guzar chuka tha.
5. Nashta bhi jaldi mein kiya aur ek zaroori kaam taqreeban reh hi gaya tha.
6. Ab soch raha hoon ke raat ko sonay se pehle mobile ek taraf rakh doon aur aglay din ke kaam pehle se tay kar loon.
7. Shayad is tarah subah ka waqt behtar guzray.

Voice 88 — Kisi ki salgirah
1. Kal mere ek dost ki salgirah thi aur hum sab ne mil kar us ke liye ek chhota sa program banaya.
2. Hum ne pehle use bataya hi nahi ke hum kya karne walay hain taake use achanak khushi ho.
3. Shaam ko jab woh aaya to sab dost pehle se wahan mojood thay.
4. Cake kaata gaya, tasveerein banayi gayin aur phir sab ne mil kar khana khaya.
5. Mere dost ko yeh surprise bohat pasand aaya.
6. Mujhe lagta hai ke kisi ko khush karne ke liye hamesha mehnga tohfa khareedna zaroori nahi hota.
7. Kabhi kabhi doston ka saath, thora sa waqt aur ek mukhlisana koshish bhi kisi ke din ko yaadgar bana deti hai.

Voice 89 — Park mein shaam
1. Yaar kal shaam main kuch der ke liye qareebi park gaya tha.
2. Poora din ghar mein baith kar kaam karne ke baad bahar nikalna acha laga.
3. Park mein bachay khel rahe thay, kuch log walk kar rahe thay aur chand dost ek bench par baithay baatein kar rahe thay.
4. Main bhi thori der chalta raha aur phir ek jagah baith kar ird gird ka mahaul dekhnay laga.
5. Sooraj aahista aahista neeche ja raha tha aur hawa bhi thandi ho gayi thi.
6. Kabhi kabhi zehan ko taza karne ke liye kisi bari tafreeh ki zaroorat nahi hoti.
7. Bas thori der bahar guzarna bhi kaafi hota hai.

Voice 90 — Mustaqbil ke mansoobay
1. Bhai har insaan apne mustaqbil ke baray mein kuch na kuch sochta hai.
2. Koi achhi naukri hasil karna chahta hai, koi apna karobar shuru karna chahta hai aur koi mazeed taleem hasil karne ka irada rakhta hai.
3. Mere khayal mein sirf khwab dekhna kaafi nahi balkay un ke liye amli qadam uthana bhi zaroori hai.
4. Agar hum kisi maqsad ko hasil karna chahtay hain to humein pehle samajhna chahiye ke is ke liye kaun si maharatein aur kitni mehnat darkar hai.
5. Phir rozana thora sa waqt is maqsad ko dena chahiye.
6. Rastay mein mushkilat zaroor aati hain, lekin mustaqil mizaji se insaan apni manzil ke qareeb pohanch sakta hai.

Voice 91 — Mehngai ka asar
1. Yaar aaj kal mehengai ki wajah se aam aadmi ke liye ghar ka kharch chalana kaafi mushkil ho gaya hai.
2. Pehle jin cheezon ko khareedna aasaan lagta tha, ab un ki qeemat dekh kar dobara sochna parta hai.
3. Ghar mein rashan khareedna ho, bijli ka bill ada karna ho ya bachon ki fees deni ho, har cheez ke liye budget banana zaroori ho gaya hai.
4. Mere khayal mein humein apni zarooriyat aur khwahishat mein farq samajhna chahiye.
5. Ghair zaroori kharch kam karne se kuch paise bach sakte hain.
6. Agar hum pehle se hisaab rakhein to mehdood aamdani mein bhi mamlaat ko behtar tareeqay se sambhal sakte hain.

Voice 92 — Computer seekhna
1. Bhai aaj kal computer ka istemal taqreeban har shobay mein hota hai, is liye is ki bunyadi samajh hona bohat zaroori hai.
2. Pehle mujhe computer par kaam karte hue kai chhoti chhoti cheezon mein mushkil pesh aati thi.
3. Kabhi file mehfooz karne mein masla hota tha aur kabhi koi program chalana samajh nahi aata tha.
4. Phir main ne rozana thori mashq shuru ki aur mukhtalif kaam khud karne ki koshish ki.
5. Aahista aahista mera aitmaad barhnay laga.
6. Mere khayal mein computer seekhne ka behtareen tareeqa sirf videos dekhna nahi balkay khud kaam karna bhi hai.
7. Ghalti ho to use samjho aur dobara koshish karo.

Voice 93 — Safar mein gam ho jana
1. Yaar ek dafa main apne dost ke saath ek nayi jagah janay nikla to rasta theek se maloom nahi tha.
2. Hum ne socha ke logon se pooch kar pohanch jayenge, lekin har shakhs alag rasta bata raha tha.
3. Phir hum ne mobile par naqsha khola aur apni manzil talash karne ki koshish ki.
4. Kuch der baad pata chala ke hum ghalat sarak par ja rahe thay.
5. Hum ne wapas mur kar sahi rasta ikhtiyar kiya aur aakhirkar apni manzil par pohanch gaye.
6. Us din mujhe ehsaas hua ke safar se pehle rasta dekh lena chahiye.
7. Thori si tayari se kaafi waqt bachaya ja sakta hai.

Voice 94 — Acha ustad
1. Yaar ek acha ustad sirf kitaab ka sabaq nahi parhata balkay talib-e-ilm ko samajhne aur sochnay ka tareeqa bhi sikhata hai.
2. Mujhe aisay ustad pasand hain jo mushkil mauzoo ko aasaan misaalon se samjhatay hain aur sawal poochhne par naraz nahi hotay.
3. Baaz auqat talib-e-ilm ko ek baat samajhne ke liye do ya teen misaalon ki zaroorat hoti hai.
4. Agar ustad sabr se dobara samjha de to talib-e-ilm ka aitmaad barh jata hai.
5. Mere khayal mein taleem ka maqsad sirf imtehan mein achhay number lena nahi hona chahiye.
6. Asal maqsad yeh hai ke insaan ilm hasil kare, use samajh sakay aur amli zindagi mein istemal bhi kar sakay.

Voice 95 — Eid ki khareedari
1. Bhai Eid se pehle bazaaron mein alag hi ronaq hoti hai.
2. Har taraf log kapray, jootay aur doosri zaroori cheezen khareedne mein masroof nazar aate hain.
3. Kuch dukaanon par itna rush hota hai ke andar jana bhi mushkil ho jata hai.
4. Pichhli Eid par main apne ghar walon ke saath khareedari karne gaya to hum ne kai dukaanen dekhin, kyun ke ek hi cheez ki qeemat har jagah mukhtalif thi.
5. Kaafi chalne phirne ke baad sab ki khareedari mukammal hui.
6. Ghar wapas aaye to thakan bhi thi, lekin sab khush thay.
7. Mere khayal mein agar khareedari pehle shuru kar di jaye to aakhri dinon ki bhaag daur se bacha ja sakta hai.

Voice 96 — Mobile ki battery
1. Yaar jab mobile ki battery kam ho aur charger saath na ho to bari pareshani hoti hai.
2. Khaas taur par jab ghar se bahar hon aur kisi zaroori shakhs se rabta karna ho.
3. Pichhlay dinon main ek kaam se bahar gaya tha aur nikalte waqt mobile charge karna bhool gaya.
4. Kuch der baad battery bohat kam reh gayi to main ne ghair zaroori apps band kar dein aur phone ka istemal bhi kam kar diya.
5. Khush qismati se zaroori call karne ke liye battery bach gayi.
6. Ab main koshish karta hoon ke ghar se nikalne se pehle mobile charge kar loon.
7. Chhoti si aadat kai masail se bacha sakti hai.

Voice 97 — Kitabon ki dukaan
1. Kal main ek kitabon ki dukaan par gaya tha kyun ke mujhe parhai ke liye ek nayi kitaab khareedni thi.
2. Dukaan mein dakhil hua to har taraf mukhtalif mauzuaat ki kitabein rakhi hui thin.
3. Kuch kitabein taleemi thin, kuch kahaniyon ke baray mein thin aur kuch zaati taraqqi se mutaliq thin.
4. Main ne dukandaar se apni zaroorat batayi to us ne mujhe chand kitabein dikhayen.
5. Main ne un ke safhay dekhay aur phir ek kitaab pasand kar li.
6. Mujhe aisi dukaanon mein waqt guzarna acha lagta hai kyun ke wahan bohat kuch naya dekhne ko milta hai.
7. Kabhi kabhi ek achhi kitaab insaan ki soch badalne ka sabab ban jati hai.

Voice 98 — Apna kamra tarteeb dena
1. Yaar mera kamra kuch dinon se kaafi bikhra hua tha, is liye main ne socha ke aaj ise achhi tarah tarteeb deta hoon.
2. Mez par kitabein, kaghaz aur doosri cheezen jama ho gayi thin.
3. Pehle main ne sara samaan ek taraf rakha, phir ghair zaroori kaghaz alag kiye aur kitabein tarteeb se rakh dein.
4. Is ke baad mez saaf ki aur kapray bhi apni jagah par rakh diye.
5. Kaam mukammal hua to kamra kaafi behtar lag raha tha.
6. Mujhe ehsaas hua ke saaf mahaul mein kaam karna aasaan hota hai aur cheezen talash karne mein bhi waqt zaya nahi hota.
7. Ab koshish karunga ke rozana thori safai karta rahoon.

Voice 99 — Khanay ka zaya
1. Bhai hamaray muashray mein khana zaya karna ek aam masla ban gaya hai.
2. Baaz auqat log apni zaroorat se zyada khana plate mein daal letay hain aur phir use mukammal nahi khatay.
3. Shadiyon aur doosri taqreebat mein bhi kaafi khana bach jata hai.
4. Mere khayal mein humein apni zaroorat ke mutabiq khana lena chahiye taake use zaya honay se bachaya ja sakay.
5. Agar ghar mein khana zyada ban jaye to use munasib tareeqay se mehfooz kiya ja sakta hai ya kisi zaroorat mand ke saath baanta ja sakta hai.
6. Khana bohat mehnat se tayyar hota hai, is liye humein is ki qadar karni chahiye aur ghair zaroori zaya se bachna chahiye.

Voice 100 — Aik masroof bazaar
1. Yaar pichhlay dinon main ek mashhoor bazaar gaya tha jahan logon ka bohat rush tha.
2. Har dukaan par khareedar mojood thay aur dukandaar apne samaan ke baray mein bata rahe thay.
3. Kahin kapray farokht ho rahe thay, kahin jootay aur kahin khanay peenay ki cheezen mil rahi thin.
4. Main ek cheez khareedne gaya tha lekin raste mein kai doosri cheezen bhi nazar aane lagin.
5. Kuch der baad ehsaas hua ke main kaafi waqt bazaar mein guzar chuka hoon.
6. Aakhirkar apni zaroorat ki cheez khareedi aur wapas aa gaya.
7. Bazaar ki ronaq achhi lagti hai, lekin rush mein ehtiyat se chalna aur apne samaan ka khayal rakhna bhi zaroori hai.

Voice 101 — Apne dost ko mashwara
1. Yaar mera ek dost pichhlay dinon ek maslay ki wajah se kaafi pareshan tha.
2. Woh baar baar isi baat ke baray mein soch raha tha aur use samajh nahi aa raha tha ke kya karna chahiye.
3. Main ne use kaha ke pehle sukoon se baith kar maslay ko samjho, phir mumkinah hal ke baray mein socho.
4. Har maslay ka hal foran nahi milta, is liye jaldbazi mein faisla karna bhi theek nahi.
5. Main ne use yeh bhi kaha ke agar zaroorat ho to kisi tajurbaykar shakhs se mashwara le le.
6. Kuch der baat karne ke baad woh pehle se behtar mehsoos kar raha tha.
7. Kabhi kabhi kisi ki baat sun lena bhi bari madad hoti hai.

Voice 102 — Nayi cycle
1. Bachpan mein mujhe cycle chalane ka bohat shauq tha.
2. Shuru mein jab main ne cycle chalana seekhna shuru kiya to kai martaba tawazun kharab hua aur main gir bhi gaya.
3. Mere baray bhai ne mujhe samjhaya ke himmat na haaro aur aahista aahista koshish karte raho.
4. Kuch dinon ki mashq ke baad main khud cycle chalane laga.
5. Phir doston ke saath galiyon mein ghoomna aur nayi jagahen dekhna bohat acha lagta tha.
6. Aaj bhi jab kahin bachay cycle chalatay nazar aate hain to mujhe apna bachpan yaad aa jata hai.
7. Kuch yaadein waqt guzarne ke bawajood insaan ke dil mein hamesha taza rehti hain.

Voice 103 — Aik achanak mansooba
1. Yaar kabhi kabhi achanak bannay walay mansoobay bhi bohat mazay ke hotay hain.
2. Pichhlay haftay hum sab dost ghar baithay baatein kar rahe thay ke ek dost ne kaha, kyun na abhi bahar chaltay hain.
3. Pehle kisi ko samajh nahi aa raha tha ke kahan jayen, phir faisla hua ke qareebi restaurant mein khana khatay hain.
4. Sab ne jaldi se tayari ki aur kuch hi der mein ikatthay ho gaye.
5. Wahan baith kar khoob baatein huin aur kai dilchasp waqiat yaad aaye.
6. Kisi ne bhi is program ki pehle se mansooba bandi nahi ki thi, lekin phir bhi shaam bohat achhi guzri.
7. Kabhi kabhi saada sa mansooba bhi yaadgar ban jata hai.

Voice 104 — Daftar ka tajurba
1. Bhai daftar mein kaam karne ka tajurba insaan ko bohat si nayi cheezen sikhata hai.
2. Wahan sirf apna kaam mukammal karna kaafi nahi hota balkay waqt ki pabandi, doosron ke saath taawun aur zimmedari ka ehsaas bhi zaroori hai.
3. Kabhi kaam zyada hota hai aur kabhi achanak koi naya kaam saamne aa jata hai.
4. Aisay waqt mein ghabranay ke bajaye kaamon ki tarjeeh tay karna behtar hota hai.
5. Agar kisi kaam mein mushkil pesh aaye to saathiyon se poochhne mein bhi koi harj nahi.
6. Mere khayal mein daftar mein kamyabi ke liye mehnat ke saath acha rawaiya aur musalsal seekhne ki aadat bhi aham kirdar ada karti hai.

Voice 105 — Pani ki ahmiyat
1. Yaar pani hamari zindagi ki bunyadi zaroorat hai, lekin hum aksar is ki qadar nahi karte.
2. Ghar mein nal khula chhor dete hain ya gaari aur sehan dhotay waqt zaroorat se zyada pani istemal karte hain.
3. Baaz ilaqon mein logon ko saaf pani hasil karne ke liye kaafi mushkilat ka saamna karna parta hai.
4. Mere khayal mein humein rozmarrah ki chhoti aadaton par tawajjo deni chahiye.
5. Daant saaf karte waqt nal band rakhna, leak honay walay nal ko theek karwana aur pani ko ehtiyat se istemal karna achhay iqdamat hain.
6. Agar har shakhs apni zimmedari samjhay to bohat sa pani bachaya ja sakta hai aur doosron ke liye bhi faida hoga.

Voice 106 — Kisi nayi jagah ka dorah
1. Pichhlay mahinay mujhe ek aisi jagah janay ka mauqa mila jahan main pehle kabhi nahi gaya tha.
2. Wahan pohanch kar sab se pehle main ne ird gird ka mahaul dekha.
3. Imaratein mukhtalif andaaz ki thin aur sarakon par bhi kaafi ronaq thi.
4. Main ne wahan ke logon se chand baatein poochheen to unhon ne khush ikhlaqi se rasta samjhaya.
5. Phir main ne qareebi dukaan se chai li aur kuch der baith kar aaram kiya.
6. Nayi jagahon par janay se insaan ko mukhtalif logon aur un ke rehan sehan ke baray mein jaanne ka mauqa milta hai.
7. Isi liye mujhe kabhi kabhi mamool se hat kar nayi jagahon ki sair karna acha lagta hai.

Voice 107 — Rozana warzish
1. Yaar main ne mehsoos kiya hai ke rozana thori warzish karne se jism mein chusti mehsoos hoti hai.
2. Pehle main zyada waqt baith kar guzarta tha aur kaam ke baad kaafi thaka hua mehsoos karta tha.
3. Phir main ne faisla kiya ke rozana kuch der chahal qadmi karunga.
4. Shuru mein yeh mamool qaim rakhna mushkil tha, lekin aahista aahista aadat banne lagi.
5. Ab koshish karta hoon ke din mein kuch waqt jismani sargarmi ke liye zaroor nikaaloon.
6. Zaroori nahi ke har shakhs mushkil warzish kare.
7. Apni istitaat ke mutabiq chalna phirna bhi acha aaghaz hai.
8. Asal baat yeh hai ke apni sehat ka khayal rakha jaye.

Voice 108 — Internet par waqt guzarna
1. Bhai kabhi kabhi hum internet par sirf paanch minute guzarne ke iraday se mobile kholtay hain, lekin phir kaafi der tak videos dekhtay rehte hain.
2. Ek video ke baad doosri video saamne aa jati hai aur humein waqt guzarne ka ehsaas nahi hota.
3. Jab mobile band karte hain to pata chalta hai ke zaroori kaam abhi tak mukammal nahi hua.
4. Mere saath bhi kai baar aisa hua hai.
5. Ab main koshish karta hoon ke mobile istemal karne se pehle tay kar loon ke kitni der istemal karna hai.
6. Internet bohat mufeed hai, lekin ise samajhdari se istemal karna chahiye taake waqt bhi zaya na ho aur zaroori kaam bhi mukammal hotay rahen.

Voice 109 — Khandan ki dawat
1. Kal hamaray khandan mein ek dawat thi aur sab rishtedaar wahan jama hue thay.
2. Kuch log kaafi arsay baad mil rahe thay, is liye har taraf baaton aur hansi ki aawazein aa rahi thin.
3. Ghar ke baray ek jagah baithay purani baatein yaad kar rahe thay, jabke bachay idhar udhar khel rahe thay.
4. Khanay ka intizam bhi acha tha aur sab ne mil kar khana khaya.
5. Mujhe aisay mauqay achhay lagtay hain kyun ke masroof zindagi mein sab ko ikattha honay ka mauqa mil jata hai.
6. Kabhi kabhi rishtedaron se rabta kam ho jata hai, lekin aisi mulaqatein talluqaat ko dobara taza karne mein madad deti hain.

Voice 110 — Khareedari mein samajhdari
1. Yaar koi bhi cheez khareedne se pehle us ke baray mein thori maloomat hasil karna faidemand hota hai.
2. Kabhi hum sirf khoobsurat packing dekh kar cheez khareed letay hain, lekin baad mein pata chalta hai ke woh hamari zaroorat ke mutabiq nahi thi.
3. Khaas taur par mehngi cheezen khareedtay waqt qeemat, mayaar aur istemal ke baray mein sochna chahiye.
4. Agar mumkin ho to mukhtalif dukaanon ki qeematein bhi dekh leni chahiye.
5. Mere khayal mein acha khareedar woh nahi jo sab se mehngi cheez khareeday, balkay woh hai jo apne paison ke badlay munasib mayaar hasil kare.
6. Soch samajh kar khareedari karne se ghair zaroori kharch kam ho sakta hai.

Voice 111 — Pehli tankhwah
1. Yaar pehli tankhwah milne ki khushi hi alag hoti hai, khaas taur par jab insaan ne is ke liye kaafi mehnat ki ho.
2. Mujhe lagta hai ke jab koi apni kamai se ghar walon ke liye kuch khareedta hai to use bohat acha mehsoos hota hai.
3. Pehli tankhwah ke baad insaan ko paison ki ahmiyat bhi zyada samajh aane lagti hai, kyun ke ab use maloom hota hai ke paise kamane ke liye waqt aur mehnat dono lagtay hain.
4. Mere khayal mein kamai shuru karne ke baad kharch aur bachat mein tawazun rakhna chahiye.
5. Zarooriyat poori karne ke saath mustaqbil ke liye kuch raqam bachana bhi achhi aadat hai.

Voice 112 — Subah ki chai
1. Bhai mere liye subah ki chai din ka ek aham hissa hai.
2. Jab main subah uth kar garam chai ka cup haath mein leta hoon to kaafi sukoon mehsoos hota hai.
3. Kabhi ghar mein sab log nashtay ke waqt ikatthay baithte hain aur chai ke saath mukhtalif baatein karte hain.
4. Kisi ko apne kaam par janay ki jaldi hoti hai aur kisi ko university pohanchna hota hai, lekin in chand minuteon mein sab ek doosray se baat kar letay hain.
5. Waise chai banane ka tareeqa bhi har ghar mein mukhtalif hota hai.
6. Kisi ko tez patti pasand hai aur kisi ko halki.
7. Asal maza to apnon ke saath baith kar chai peenay mein hai.

Voice 113 — Imtehan ka nateeja
1. Yaar imtehan ka nateeja aane se pehle har talib-e-ilm ke dil mein mukhtalif khayalat hotay hain.
2. Koi achhay numberon ki umeed kar raha hota hai aur koi soch raha hota hai ke kahin kisi mazmoon mein kam number na aa jayen.
3. Jab nateeja saamne aata hai to kuch log khush hotay hain aur kuch ko mayoosi hoti hai.
4. Mere khayal mein nateeja aham zaroor hai, lekin yeh insaan ki poori qabiliyat ka faisla nahi karta.
5. Agar number achhay aayen to mehnat jari rakhni chahiye, aur agar kam aayen to apni ghaltiyon ko samajh kar agli baar behtar tayari karni chahiye.
6. Asal kamyabi apni ghaltiyon se seekhne aur aagay barhne mein hai.

Voice 114 — Khandan ke saath safar
1. Pichhli chhuttiyon mein hum sab ghar walay ek saath safar par gaye thay.
2. Subah sawairay ghar se niklay taake raste mein zyada rush na milay.
3. Shuru mein sab log kaafi purjosh thay aur gaari mein mukhtalif baatein kar rahe thay.
4. Raste mein ek jagah rukay, chai pi aur kuch der aaram kiya.
5. Safar ke dauran bahar ke manazir dekhna bhi acha lag raha tha.
6. Manzil par pohanch kar hum ne tasveerein banayin aur mukhtalif jagahen dekhin.
7. Wapsi par sab thakay hue thay, lekin khushi bhi thi ke hum ne acha waqt guzara.
8. Mere khayal mein khandan ke saath safar karne se yaadgar lamhaat bantay hain aur rishtay mazeed mazboot hotay hain.

Voice 115 — Apna karobar
1. Yaar bohat se log apni naukri ke saath apna karobar shuru karne ka khwab bhi dekhtay hain.
2. Karobar karne mein azadi to hoti hai, lekin is ke saath bari zimmedari bhi aati hai.
3. Sab se pehle yeh samajhna zaroori hai ke log kis cheez ki zaroorat mehsoos karte hain aur is ke liye kitne paise kharch kar sakte hain.
4. Phir karobar ke ikhrajaat, samaan aur grahakon ke baray mein mansooba bandi karni parti hai.
5. Shuru mein munafa kam bhi ho sakta hai, is liye sabr karna zaroori hai.
6. Mere khayal mein kamyab karobar ke liye achhi mansooba bandi, imandari aur grahakon ke saath acha rawaiya bohat aham hain.

Voice 116 — Bijli ka bill
1. Yaar pichhlay dinon jab bijli ka bill aaya to ghar mein sab log us ki raqam dekh kar hairan reh gaye.
2. Garmiyon mein pankhay aur doosri cheezen zyada istemal hoti hain, is liye bijli ka kharch bhi barh jata hai.
3. Hum ne socha ke kuch aadatein badalni chahiye taake ghair zaroori bijli istemal na ho.
4. Ab koshish karte hain ke kamray se bahar nikalte waqt light band kar dein aur din ke waqt jahan mumkin ho qudrati roshni istemal karein.
5. Chhoti chhoti tabdeeliyan foran bohat bara farq na bhi daalein, phir bhi waqt ke saath faida ho sakta hai.
6. Bijli bachana paise bachane ke saath wasail ki hifazat bhi hai.

Voice 117 — Baarabar shaap ka tajurba
1. Bhai kabhi kabhi baal katwanay ke liye dukaan par jana bhi ek dilchasp tajurba ban jata hai.
2. Pichhlay haftay main apne mamool ke hajjam ke paas gaya to wahan kaafi rush tha.
3. Main ne socha ke thori der intezar kar leta hoon.
4. Is dauran wahan baithay log mukhtalif baatein kar rahe thay, koi cricket par guftagu kar raha tha aur koi shehar ke halaat ke baray mein bata raha tha.
5. Jab meri baari aayi to main ne hajjam ko bataya ke baal kis tarah katwanay hain.
6. Kaam mukammal hua to aainay mein dekh kar itminan hua ke baal theek katay hain.
7. Baaz auqat rozmarrah ke aisay chhotay kaam bhi achhi yaad ban jatay hain.

Voice 118 — Ghar mein poday lagana
1. Yaar hamaray ghar mein kuch gamlay rakhay hue hain jin mein mukhtalif poday lagay hain.
2. Shuru mein mujhe podon ki dekh bhaal ka zyada tajurba nahi tha, is liye kabhi pani kam deta tha aur kabhi zaroorat se zyada.
3. Phir ghar walon ne samjhaya ke har poday ki zaroorat mukhtalif hoti hai.
4. Ab main koshish karta hoon ke waqt par pani doon, sookhay pattay hataoon aur podon ko munasib roshni milti rahay.
5. Jab naye pattay nikalte hain to bohat acha mehsoos hota hai.
6. Mere khayal mein ghar mein poday lagane se mahaul bhi khoobsurat hota hai aur insaan ko un ki dekh bhaal karte hue sabr aur zimmedari ka ehsaas bhi hota hai.

Voice 119 — Aik mushkil faisla
1. Yaar zindagi mein kabhi kabhi aisay faislay karne partay hain jin ke baray mein insaan kaafi der tak sochta rehta hai.
2. Ek taraf ek mauqa acha lag raha hota hai aur doosri taraf kuch khadshaat bhi hotay hain.
3. Aisay waqt mein sirf jazbaat ki bunyaad par faisla karne ke bajaye faiday aur nuqsan dono dekhnay chahiye.
4. Kisi samajhdar shakhs se mashwara lena bhi madadgar sabit ho sakta hai, lekin aakhir mein apni surat-e-haal ko samajh kar faisla karna zaroori hai.
5. Har faisla sau feesad durust nahi hota, is liye ghalti ka imkaan bhi qabool karna chahiye.
6. Mere khayal mein acha faisla woh hai jo soch samajh kar kiya jaye aur jis ki zimmedari lene ke liye insaan tayyar ho.

Voice 120 — Ghar mein mehmaan daari
1. Bhai hamaray haan mehmanon ki khatirdaari ko khaas ahmiyat di jati hai.
2. Jab koi rishtedaar ya dost ghar aata hai to koshish ki jati hai ke use aaram se bithaya jaye aur chai ya khanay ka intizam kiya jaye.
3. Kabhi mehmaan achanak aa jatay hain, is liye ghar walon ko foran tayari karni parti hai.
4. Mujhe acha lagta hai jab sab log mil kar mehmanon ki khidmat karte hain.
5. Mehman nawazi ka matlab sirf acha khana pesh karna nahi balkay khush ikhlaqi se pesh aana aur doosray shakhs ko izzat dena bhi hai.
6. Mere khayal mein khuloos se ki gayi chhoti si khatirdaari bhi kisi ko khush kar sakti hai.

Voice 121 — Apna waqt manazam karna
1. Yaar jab din ke kaam pehle se tarteeb na diye jayen to aksar zaroori kaam bhi reh jatay hain.
2. Mere saath pehle aisa hota tha ke subah uth kar faisla karta tha ke aaj kya karna hai, lekin phir kabhi koi phone aa jata aur kabhi koi doosra kaam saamne aa jata.
3. Shaam tak masroof rehne ke bawajood kai kaam mukammal nahi hotay thay.
4. Ab koshish karta hoon ke raat ko aglay din ke aham kaam likh loon.
5. Phir un kaamon ko ahmiyat ke mutabiq tarteeb deta hoon taake pehle zaroori cheezen mukammal ho jayen.
6. Har din mansoobay ke mutabiq nahi guzarta, lekin thori si mansooba bandi se waqt ko behtar istemal kiya ja sakta hai.

Voice 122 — Online taleem
1. Bhai aaj kal online taleem ki wajah se ghar baithay bohat kuch seekhna mumkin ho gaya hai.
2. Mukhtalif websites par courses, videos aur mashqein mojood hoti hain jin ki madad se insaan apni maharat behtar bana sakta hai.
3. Is ka faida yeh hai ke apni sahoolat ke mutabiq waqt nikaal kar parh sakte hain.
4. Lekin ek masla yeh bhi hai ke ghar mein baith kar tawajjo barqarar rakhna kabhi kabhi mushkil ho jata hai.
5. Mobile par doosri cheezen dekhne ka dil karta hai aur parhai peechay reh jati hai.
6. Mere khayal mein online taleem se faida uthane ke liye baqaida waqt muqarrar karna aur rozana mashq karna zaroori hai.

Voice 123 — Dost se narazgi
1. Yaar kabhi kabhi achhay doston ke darmiyan bhi ghalat fehmi paida ho jati hai.
2. Ek dafa mera ek dost mujh se naraz ho gaya kyun ke use laga ke main ne jaan boojh kar us ki baat nazar andaaz ki hai.
3. Haqeeqat mein main ek zaroori kaam mein masroof tha aur us ka paigham waqt par nahi dekh saka tha.
4. Jab mujhe pata chala to main ne use phone kiya aur saari baat samjhayi.
5. Is ke baad hamari ghalat fehmi door ho gayi.
6. Is waqaye se mujhe samajh aaya ke kisi baat ka matlab khud se nikalne ke bajaye saamne walay se pooch lena behtar hai.
7. Saaf guftagu bohat se masail ko barhne se rok sakti hai.

Voice 124 — Chhoti kamyabi
1. Yaar jab insaan kisi mushkil kaam ko mukammal karta hai to use ek alag tarah ki khushi mehsoos hoti hai.
2. Zaroori nahi ke kamyabi hamesha bohat bari ho.
3. Kabhi koi naya kaam seekh lena, koi mushkil sawal hal kar lena ya waqt par apni zimmedari poori kar dena bhi bari baat hoti hai.
4. Pichhlay dinon main ne ek aisa kaam mukammal kiya jise main kaafi arsay se taal raha tha.
5. Kaam khatam hua to zehan se ek bojh sa utar gaya.
6. Is tajurbe ne mujhe sikhaya ke mushkil kaam ko chhotay hisson mein taqseem karke shuru karna chahiye.
7. Jab pehla qadam utha liya jaye to aagay barhna nisbat-an aasaan ho jata hai.

Voice 125 — Apne shehar ki safai
1. Yaar shehar ko saaf rakhna sirf hukoomat ki zimmedari nahi balkay har shehri ka bhi farz hai.
2. Aksar hum sarak par kachra phenk dete hain aur phir shikayat karte hain ke hamara ilaqa saaf nahi rehta.
3. Agar har shakhs apni zimmedari samjhay aur kachra munasib jagah par daalay to mahaul kaafi behtar ho sakta hai.
4. Dukandaron, rehaishiyon aur idaron sab ko is maamlay mein taawun karna chahiye.
5. Safai se na sirf shehar khoobsurat lagta hai balkay logon ke liye mahaul bhi behtar rehta hai.
6. Mere khayal mein bachon ko bhi shuru se safai ki ahmiyat samjhani chahiye taake woh bari umar mein achhi aadatein barqarar rakh sakein.

Voice 126 — Aane walay dinon ki umeed
1. Yaar zindagi mein har din ek jaisa nahi hota.
2. Kabhi kaam aasani se mukammal ho jatay hain aur kabhi chhoti chhoti baatein bhi pareshani paida kar deti hain.
3. Aisay waqt mein yeh sochna chahiye ke mushkil halaat hamesha nahi rehte aur koshish se behtari laayi ja sakti hai.
4. Main bhi koshish karta hoon ke pichhlay din ki ghaltiyon se seekhun aur aglay din ke liye behtar mansooba banaoon.
5. Agar koi kaam mukammal na ho sakay to mayoos honay ke bajaye dobara koshish karni chahiye.
6. Mere khayal mein umeed rakhne ka matlab sirf achhay waqt ka intezar karna nahi balkay apne halaat behtar banane ke liye amli qadam uthana bhi hai.

Voice 127
1. Yaar, kabhi kabhi main sochta hoon ke zindagi kitni ajeeb hai.
2. Jab hum chhotay hotay hain to humein lagta hai ke baray ho kar hamari zindagi bohat aasaan ho jayegi, hum jo chahein ge woh karein ge, jahan chahein ge jayen ge, aur kisi ki rok tok bhi nahi hogi.
3. Lekin jab insaan bara hota hai to usay pata chalta hai ke asal zindagi to zimmedariyon, pareshaniyon aur faislon ka naam hai.
4. Kabhi parhai ka dabao hota hai, kabhi kaam ki tension, kabhi ghar ke masail, aur kabhi apne mustaqbil ki fikar insaan ko sukoon se baithne bhi nahi deti.

Voice 128
1. Lekin bhai, mere khayal mein zindagi ka asal maza tab aata hai jab insaan in sab cheezon ke bawajood apne liye thora sa waqt nikalta hai.
2. Zaroori nahi ke har din bohat acha guzray ya har kaam hamari marzi ke mutabiq ho.
3. Kuch din aisay bhi hotay hain jab insaan poori koshish karta hai, lekin phir bhi nateeja uski tawaqqo ke mutabiq nahi aata.
4. Aisay waqt mein khud ko nakaam samajhne ke bajaye yeh sochna chahiye ke shayad abhi mazeed mehnat ki zaroorat hai.
5. Waise bhi, har insaan ki zindagi ka apna aik waqt hota hai.
6. Koi jaldi kamyab ho jata hai aur kisi ko apni manzil tak pohanchne mein zyada waqt lagta hai.

Voice 129
1. Iska matlab yeh nahi ke jo dair se kamyab ho raha hai woh kam qabil hai.
2. Yaar, agar tum kisi doosray shakhs ki zindagi dekh kar apni zindagi se mayoos ho rahay ho to aik baat yaad rakhna ke tumhein uski kamyabi nazar aati hai, lekin uske peechay ki mehnat, pareshaniyan aur nakaamiyan shayad nazar nahi aateen.
3. Is liye doosron se muqabla karne ke bajaye khud ko behtar banane ki koshish karo.
4. Agar aaj tum kal se thora zyada jaantay ho, koi nayi cheez seekh rahay ho ya apni kisi buri aadat ko chhorne ki koshish kar rahay ho to yeh bhi aik kamyabi hai.

Voice 130
1. Mujhe lagta hai ke insaan ko apne khwab zaroor baray rakhne chahiye, lekin un khwabon ko poora karne ke liye chhotay chhotay qadam uthana bhi seekhna chahiye.
2. Sirf sochnay se kuch nahi badalta, asal farq tab parta hai jab hum rozana thora sa kaam karte hain.
3. Chahe tum parh rahay ho, naukri kar rahay ho ya apna koi karobar shuru karna chahtay ho, sab se zaroori cheez mustaqil mizaji hai.
4. Kabhi dil nahi karega, kabhi thakan hogi, kabhi log tumhari hausla shikni karein ge, lekin tumhein khud faisla karna hai ke tum ne kahan rukna hai aur kahan aagay barhna hai.

Voice 131
1. Aur haan, is safar mein apni sehat, apne ghar walon aur apne doston ko bhi nazar andaaz mat karna.
2. Paisa aur kamyabi zaroori hain, lekin zehni sukoon aur achay rishtay bhi zindagi ka aham hissa hain.
3. Aakhir mein, main yahi kahoon ga ke zindagi ko doosron ke mayaar ke mutabiq guzarne ke bajaye apni zarooriyat, apni khushiyon aur apne maqsad ko samajhne ki koshish karo.
4. Ghaltiyan hongi, faislay ghalat bhi ho sakte hain, lekin har ghalti se kuch na kuch seekhne ko zaroor milta hai.
5. Bas yaar, khud par yaqeen rakho, waqt ki qadar karo, apne kaam se imandari rakho aur aahista aahista aagay barhtay raho.
6. Zaroori nahi ke tum aik hi din mein apni poori zindagi badal do, kabhi kabhi aik chhoti si achi aadat bhi mustaqbil mein bohat bara farq paida kar deti hai.

Voice 132
1. Yaar, pata hai kabhi kabhi main akela baith kar sochta hoon ke hamari zindagi bhi kitni ajeeb hai.
2. Hum har roz subah uthte hain, apne kaamon mein masroof ho jatay hain, kisi ko parhai ki fikar hoti hai, kisi ko naukri ki, kisi ko ghar ke ikhrajaat poore karne hotay hain, aur kisi ko apne mustaqbil ki pareshani lagi rehti hai.
3. Din guzartay jatay hain, maheenay guzar jatay hain, saal bhi guzar jatay hain, lekin kabhi kabhi hum ruk kar yeh sochtay hi nahi ke aakhir hum apni zindagi mein kar kya rahay hain.

Voice 133
1. Hum subah se shaam tak masroof to rehtay hain, lekin kya hum waqai us simt mein ja rahay hain jahan hum pohanchna chahtay hain?
2. Bhai, mere khayal mein yeh sawal har insaan ko kabhi na kabhi khud se zaroor poochna chahiye, kyun ke masroof rehna aur sahi simt mein aagay barhna dono alag alag cheezein hain.
3. Misaal ke taur par, agar koi shakhs sara din kaam karta hai, bohat zyada thak jata hai, lekin usay yeh bhi maloom nahi ke woh apni zindagi mein kya hasil karna chahta hai, to mumkin hai ke kuch arsay baad woh khud ko aik hi jagah khara mehsoos kare.

Voice 134
1. Doosri taraf, agar koi shakhs rozana thora sa waqt nikal kar apne maqsad ke liye kaam karta hai, nayi cheezein seekhta hai, apni ghaltiyon ko samajhta hai aur apni aadaton ko behtar banata hai, to aahista aahista uski zindagi mein tabdeeli aana shuru ho jati hai.
2. Zaroori nahi ke woh tabdeeli pehle din hi nazar aane lagay, lekin waqt ke saath uska asar zaroor samne aata hai.
3. Yaar, yahi baat mujhe bohat aham lagti hai ke hum aksar fori nataij chahtay hain.
4. Hum koi nayi cheez seekhna shuru karte hain to chand din baad sochnay lagtay hain ke abhi tak humein sab kuch kyun nahi aata.

Voice 135
1. Hum koi karobar shuru karte hain to chahtay hain ke pehle maheenay hi bohat zyada paisay kamana shuru kar dein.
2. Hum naukri ke liye koshish karte hain to chand darkhwastein bhejne ke baad mayoos ho jatay hain.
3. Lekin bhai, haqeeqat yeh hai ke zyada tar achi cheezon ko waqt lagta hai.
4. Agar tum kisi darakht ko aaj pani do ge to zaroori nahi ke kal us par phal lag jayen.
5. Pehle uski jarain mazboot hongi, phir uska tana barhay ga, shaakhein niklein gi aur waqt aane par phal bhi lagein ge.
6. Insaan ki zindagi bhi kuch aisi hi hai.

Voice 136
1. Tum aaj jo kuch seekh rahay ho, jo mehnat kar rahay ho, jo chhoti chhoti ghaltiyan kar ke un se sabaq hasil kar rahay ho, mumkin hai ke in sab ka faida tumhein kai maheenon ya saalon baad samajh aaye.
2. Is liye har cheez ka nateeja foran dekhne ki aadat chhor deni chahiye.
3. Haan, iska matlab yeh bhi nahi ke insaan baghair sochay samjhay barson tak aik hi kaam karta rahe.
4. Waqtan fawaqtan yeh dekhna bhi zaroori hai ke tumhari koshish sahi simt mein ja rahi hai ya nahi.
5. Agar koi tareeqa kaam nahi kar raha to usay badalne mein koi burai nahi.

Voice 137
1. Asal baat yeh hai ke apne maqsad ko samjho, apne tareeqay ko behtar banao aur zaroorat parne par apni ghalti maanne se bhi na ghabrao.
2. Mujhe lagta hai ke zindagi mein insaan ki sab se bari taqat sirf uski zehant nahi balkay seekhtay rehne ki aadat bhi hai.
3. Bohat se log shuru mein kisi kaam mein zyada achay nahi hotay, lekin woh musalsal koshish karte rehtay hain, sawal poochte hain, doosron se seekhtay hain aur aahista aahista behtar hotay jatay hain.
4. Iske baraks kuch log sirf is liye aagay nahi barh patay kyun ke unhein lagta hai ke agar unhon ne koi sawal pooch liya to log unhein kam aql samjhein ge.

Voice 138
1. Aray bhai, koi bhi insaan har cheez jaan kar paida nahi hota.
2. Hum sab ko kahin na kahin se seekhna parta hai.
3. Agar tumhein koi baat samajh nahi aa rahi to pooch lo, agar kisi kaam mein ghalti ho gayi hai to usay theek karna seekh lo, aur agar koi shakhs tum se behtar kaam kar raha hai to us se jalne ke bajaye yeh samajhne ki koshish karo ke woh aisa kya kar raha hai jo tum nahi kar rahay.

Voice 139
1. Kisi ki kamyabi se seekhna tumhari apni kamyabi ka rasta bhi aasaan kar sakta hai.
2. Waise aik baat aur hai, hum mein se bohat se log apni zindagi ka bara hissa doosron ko dekhne mein guzar detay hain.
3. Kisi ne nayi gari khareed li to humein apni zindagi kam achi lagne lagti hai, kisi ne naya ghar bana liya to humein apni mehnat befaida mehsoos hone lagti hai.

Voice 140
1. Aur agar kisi dost ko achi naukri mil jaye to hum apne mustaqbil ke baray mein pareshan hona shuru ho jatay hain.
2. Lekin yaar, zara socho, kya yeh zaroori hai ke sab log aik hi waqt mein aik hi maqam par pohnchein?
3. Bilkul nahi.

Voice 141 — Sachaa dost kaun hota hai?
1. Asal dost woh hota hai jis ke samne tum apne dil ki baat keh sako, apni pareshani bata sako aur apni ghalti tasleem kar sako, baghair is khauf ke ke woh har baat ka mazaq banaye ga.
2. Aisa dost tumhari khushi mein khush hota hai, tumhein aagay barhne ki targheeb deta hai aur jab tum kisi mushkil mein hotay ho to kam az kam tumhari baat sunne ke liye waqt zaroor nikalta hai.

Voice 142 — Dosti mein samajh boojh
1. Lekin yaar, dosti sirf yeh nahi ke doosra shakhs har waqt tumhare liye mojood rahe.
2. Tumhein bhi uske jazbaat aur waqt ki qadar karni chahiye.
3. Kabhi tumhara dost masroof ho sakta hai, kabhi woh kisi pareshani mein ho sakta hai, aur kabhi usay akelay baithne ki zaroorat bhi ho sakti hai.
4. Har baat ko dil par lena ya yeh samajh lena ke jawab dair se aaya hai to samne wala tumhein ahmiyat nahi deta, hamesha durust nahi hota.
5. Har insaan ki apni zindagi aur apni zimmedariyan hoti hain.

Voice 143 — Rishton mein baat cheet ki ahmiyat
1. Achay talluqaat ke liye aitmaad, samajh boojh aur baat cheet bohat zaroori hain.
2. Agar koi baat buri lagi hai to usay dil mein rakhne ke bajaye munasib waqt par sukoon se baat kar lo.
3. Ho sakta hai ke samne walay ka maqsad tumhein takleef pohanchana hi na ho.
4. Aur agar koi shakhs baar baar tumhari beizzati karta hai, tumhari baaton ko ahmiyat nahi deta ya sirf apne matlab ke waqt tumhein yaad karta hai, to phir tumhein yeh bhi sochna chahiye ke is talluq mein tumhare liye kitni jagah hai.

Voice 144 — Apni izzat aur zehni sukoon
1. Har rishta zabardasti barqarar rakhna zaroori nahi hota.
2. Kabhi kabhi munasib faasla rakhna bhi zehni sukoon ke liye zaroori hota hai.
3. Iska matlab yeh nahi ke tum kisi se nafrat karo, balkay iska matlab yeh hai ke tum apni izzat aur apne sukoon ki ahmiyat bhi samjho.
4. Isi tarah ghar walon ke saath talluqaat bhi bohat aham hain.
5. Kabhi kabhi hum bahar ke logon ke samne bohat achay tareeqay se baat karte hain, lekin ghar aa kar chhoti chhoti baaton par ghussa karne lagtay hain.

Voice 145 — Ghar walon ke liye waqt
1. Hum yeh bhool jatay hain ke ghar walon ko bhi hamari tawajjo, waqt aur achay rawaiye ki zaroorat hoti hai.
2. Agar tum poora din masroof rehtay ho to koshish karo ke ghar aa kar kuch waqt unke saath bhi guzaro.
3. Zaroori nahi ke tum unhein koi mehnga tohfa do ya har roz unke liye kuch khareed kar lao.
4. Kabhi unke paas baith kar baatein kar lena, unka haal pooch lena ya kisi chhotay se kaam mein unki madad kar dena bhi bohat maani rakhta hai.

Voice 146 — Kamyabi aur rishton mein tawazun
1. Baaz auqaat insaan ko baad mein ehsaas hota hai ke jin logon ke liye woh mehnat kar raha tha, unhi logon ke saath baithne ka waqt usne bohat kam nikala.
2. Is liye kamyabi ke peechay bhagtay hue un logon ko mat bhoolo jo tumhare mushkil waqt mein tumhare saath kharay rahay.
3. Waise bhi zindagi mein har cheez paisay se nahi khareedi ja sakti.
4. Tum achi gari khareed saktay ho, acha phone le saktay ho, baray ghar mein reh saktay ho, lekin in cheezon se laziman dil ka sukoon nahi milta.

Voice 147 — Dil ka sukoon
1. Sukoon ka talluq is baat se bhi hai ke tum apne aap se kitne mutmain ho, tumhare talluqaat kaise hain, tum apne waqt ko kaise istemal karte ho aur tumhein apni zindagi mein kya cheez aham lagti hai.
2. Ab agar main apne rozmarra ke mamoolaat ki baat karoon to mujhe lagta hai ke hum aksar apne waqt ki qadar us waqt samajhtay hain jab waqt hamare haath se nikal chuka hota hai.
3. Hum kehte hain ke abhi to bohat waqt hai, yeh kaam kal kar lein ge, parhai aglay haftay shuru karein ge, warzish aglay maheenay se karein ge.

Voice 148 — Kal par kaam chhorne ki aadat
1. Aur kisi zaroori shakhs ko phone karne ke liye bhi kehte rehtay hain ke farigh ho kar baat karein ge.
2. Lekin masla yeh hai ke woh farigh waqt aksar aata hi nahi.
3. Aik kaam khatam hota hai to doosra samne aa jata hai, aur phir din khatam ho jata hai.
4. Is liye har aham kaam ke liye kisi behtareen waqt ka intezar karne ke bajaye thora sa waqt abhi nikalna zyada faidemand hai.
5. Agar tum rozana sirf bees minute kisi nayi maharat ko detay ho to aik maheenay mein tum ne kai ghantay mashq kar li hogi.

Voice 149 — Chhotay qadam, baray nataij
1. Agar tum rozana thori dair paidal chaltay ho to aahista aahista yeh aik achi aadat ban sakti hai.
2. Agar tum rozana kuch paisay bachate ho to waqt ke saath aik munasib raqam jama ho sakti hai.
3. Chhotay kaam zahiri taur par mamooli lagtay hain, lekin jab woh musalsal kiye jayen to unka asar bara ho sakta hai.
4. Iske liye zaroori hai ke tum apne mamoolaat ko itna mushkil na banao ke chand din baad hi unhein chhor do.

Voice 150 — Achi aadatein kaise shuru karein?
1. Agar tum pehle bilkul warzish nahi karte to pehle din do ghantay warzish karne ka mansooba banane ke bajaye das ya pandrah minute se shuru karo.
2. Agar tum ne pehle kabhi kitaab nahi parhi to rozana chand safhay parhne ki aadat banao.
3. Agar tum kisi technical maharat ko seekh rahay ho to aik hi din mein das mozuaat mukammal karne ki koshish mat karo.
4. Aik mauzoo samjho, uski mashq karo, phir aglay mauzoo par jao.

Voice 151 — Mansooba kam, amal zyada
1. Yaar, hum aksar apni zindagi ko behtar banane ke liye itna bara mansooba bana letay hain ke us mansoobay ko shuru karna hi mushkil ho jata hai.
2. Halanke kai martaba aik chhota sa qadam uthana, aik baray mansoobay ko sirf sochtay rehne se kahin behtar hota hai.
3. Aik aur aham baat yeh hai ke aaram karna bhi zaroori hai.
4. Har waqt kaam karte rehna koi kamyabi nahi, khaas taur par agar iski wajah se tumhari neend kharab ho rahi ho, tabiyat thaki rehti ho ya tum har waqt chirchiray rehne lago.

Voice 152 — Aaram bhi zaroori hai
1. Insaan machine nahi hai ke usay musalsal chalaya jaye aur woh kabhi na thakay.
2. Agar tumhein aaram ki zaroorat hai to apne mamool mein uske liye jagah rakho.
3. Poori neend lene, munasib khana khane, kuch dair bahar nikalne aur apne pasandeeda kaam karne se bhi insaan khud ko behtar mehsoos kar sakta hai.
4. Kabhi kabhi thori dair mobile se door rehna bhi acha hota hai, kyun ke hum din bhar doosron ki zindagiyon ki tasveerein, videos aur khabrein dekhtay rehtay hain.

Voice 153 — Mobile aur waqt ka istemal
1. Aik video se doosri video kholte hue pata hi nahi chalta ke aadha ghanta kab guzar gaya.
2. Phir jab apne zaroori kaamon ka waqt aata hai to humein lagta hai ke aaj bhi waqt nahi mila.
3. Halanke waqt mojood tha, lekin hum ne usay kisi aur cheez par kharch kar diya.
4. Is liye mobile istemal karne mein bhi thora tawazun hona chahiye.
5. Iska matlab yeh nahi ke social media bilkul chhor do, balkay yeh hai ke tum khud faisla karo ke kab aur kitni dair usay istemal karna hai.

Voice 154 — Tawajjo behtar banane ki aadat
1. Agar tum kisi maqsad ke liye internet istemal kar rahay ho to koshish karo ke ghair zaroori cheezon mein ulajhne ke bajaye apna kaam mukammal karo.
2. Isi tarah apni tawajjo ko behtar banana bhi aik aham maharat hai.
3. Jab tum koi kaam kar rahay ho to baar baar notification dekhne, har chand minute baad phone uthane ya aik saath bohat se kaam karne se bachne ki koshish karo.
4. Aik waqt mein aik kaam par tawajjo dene se aksar kaam zyada behtar andaaz mein mukammal ho jata hai.

Voice 155 — Mustaqbil ke liye tayari
1. Acha bhai, ab zara mustaqbil ke baray mein bhi sochtay hain.
2. Hum sab chahtay hain ke aane walay saalon mein hamari zindagi behtar ho, hamare paas acha kaam ho, hum apne ghar walon ki madad kar sakein aur apni zarooriyat ke liye doosron par inhisaar na karna paray.
3. Lekin mustaqbil behtar banane ke liye sirf khwahish kaafi nahi hoti.
4. Humein apni maharaton par kaam karna parta hai, nayi cheezein seekhni parti hain aur apne faislon ki zimmedari bhi qabool karni parti hai.

Voice 156 — Parhai ko amli zindagi se joranaa
1. Agar tum talib-e-ilm ho to sirf imtehan paas karne ke baray mein na socho, balkay yeh bhi dekho ke tum jo kuch parh rahay ho, usay amli zindagi mein kaise istemal kar saktay ho.
2. Agar tum computer ke shobay mein jana chahtay ho to sirf videos dekhne ke bajaye khud chhotay chhotay mansoobay banao.
3. Agar tum kisi karobar mein dilchaspi rakhtay ho to apne ird gird logon ki zarooriyat ko samjho aur dekho ke tum unke liye kya behtar kar saktay ho.

Voice 157 — Maharat ke saath acha rawaiya
1. Agar tum achi naukri chahtay ho to apni maharat ke saath baat cheet, waqt ki pabandi aur zimmedari se kaam karne ki aadat bhi behtar banao.
2. Kai martaba log sirf is baat par tawajjo dete hain ke unhein kaun si nayi technology seekhni chahiye, lekin yeh bhool jatay hain ke apne kaam ko wazeh andaaz mein samjhana, team ke saath kaam karna aur masail ka hal talash karna bhi bohat aham hai.
3. Duniya mein nayi cheezein musalsal aa rahi hain, aaj jo technology bohat maqbool hai, mumkin hai kuch saal baad uski jagah koi aur cheez le le.

Voice 158 — Bunyadi usool seekhna

2. Jab tumhein kisi kaam ke peechay mojood usool samajh aa jatay hain to nayi technology seekhna nisbatan aasaan ho jata hai.
3. Lekin yaar, yahan bhi aik masla hai.
4. Hum kabhi kabhi itne zyada courses aur videos dekhte hain ke amli kaam karne ka waqt hi nahi bachta.
5. Humein lagta hai ke pehle har cheez mukammal seekh lein, phir koi mansooba banayein ge.

Voice 159 — Mansoobay bana kar seekhna
1. Halanke seekhne ka behtareen tareeqa aksar yeh hota hai ke tum aik chhota sa mansooba shuru karo, jahan koi masla aaye wahan mutalliq cheez seekho, phir dobara apne kaam par wapas aa jao.
2. Is tarah tumhein yeh bhi pata chalta hai ke tumhein asal mein kis cheez ki zaroorat hai.
3. Misaal ke taur par, agar tum aik saada sa record management system bana rahay ho to is dauran tumhein programming, data mehfooz karne, ghaltiyon ko sambhalne aur sarif ke liye aasaan nizaam banane ke baray mein amli samaj hasil ho sakti hai.

Voice 160 — Ghaltiyon se seekhna
1. Baad mein jab tum koi bara mansooba banao ge to tumhare paas sirf nazriyati maloomat nahi hongi balkay tajurba bhi hoga.
2. Aur agar pehli koshish mein tumhara mansooba sahi tareeqay se kaam nahi karta to pareshan hone ki zaroorat nahi.
3. Programming mein ghaltiyan aana aam baat hai.
4. Kabhi aik chhota sa harf ghalat hota hai, kabhi data ki qisam durust nahi hoti aur kabhi maslay ki asal wajah hamari tawaqqo se bilkul mukhtalif hoti hai.

Voice 161 — Maslay ka hal talash karna
1. Aisay waqt mein foran yeh na socho ke tum se yeh kaam nahi hoga.
2. Pehle maslay ko samjho, ghalti ka paigham parho, mukhtalif imkanat par ghaur karo aur aik aik kar ke cheezon ko check karo.
3. Yahi tareeqa aahista aahista tumhari masla hal karne ki salahiyat behtar banata hai.
4. Asal maharat sirf yeh nahi ke tum sahi code likh sako, balkay yeh bhi hai ke jab code ghalat ho jaye to tum maslay ki wajah talash kar sako.

Voice 162 — Har mushkil se sabaq lena
1. Yahi baat zindagi ke doosray mamlaat par bhi laagu hoti hai.
2. Agar koi mansooba nakaam ho jaye to us se yeh seekhne ki koshish karo ke kya cheez behtar ho sakti thi.
3. Agar tum kisi mauqay ko hasil nahi kar sakay to dekho ke agli martaba apni tayari kaise behtar bana saktay ho.
4. Agar kisi se ikhtilaf ho jaye to yeh socho ke tum apni baat zyada wazeh aur behtar andaaz mein kaise samjha saktay thay.
5. Har mushkil ko apni shakhsiyat par hamla samajhne ke bajaye usay seekhne ka mauqa samajhna bohat faidemand ho sakta hai.

Voice 166
